  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要对每个参数化的哈密顿量或李超算符独立重新计算多维光谱，计算成本高昂，限制了局部参数探索、灵敏度分析和逆向设计等应用的效率。

Method: 将前向模式自动微分与Duhamel输运理论结合：自动微分生成一、二、三阶参数依赖李超算符的导数，将其组装成路径输运算子（pathway transport operator），将参考模型的非线性响应映射到邻近系统。在对称性为su(2)×su(2)的四能级激子二聚体上进行验证，比较二、三阶输运路径算子与直接计算得到的精确投影输运矩阵。

Result: 三阶展开能准确复现投影输运算子及其相关的路径混合行为；输运算子展示了相干和耗散扰动如何在双面Feynman路径间重新分配振幅，揭示了非线性光谱中不直接可见的机理信息。

Conclusion: SAKE建立了非线性光谱的可微分计算框架，支持高效的局部参数探索、灵敏度分析以及未来的逆向设计应用，同时提供了超越光谱本身的机理洞察能力。

Abstract: We introduce the Spectral Autodiff Kernel Expansion (SAKE), a differentiable computational framework for transporting nonlinear spectroscopic response between neighboring quantum dynamical models. Rather than recomputing multidimensional spectra independently for each Hamiltonian or Liouvillian, SAKE constructs local transport expansions about a reference model by combining forward-mode automatic differentiation with Duhamel transport theory. Automatic differentiation generates first-, second-, and third-order derivatives of the parameter-dependent Liouvillian, which are assembled into a pathway transport operator that maps the nonlinear response of a reference model onto neighboring systems. The framework is validated for a four-level excitonic dimer possessing an $su(2)\times su(2)$ symmetry by comparing second- and third-order transported pathway operators with exact projected transport matrices obtained from direct calculations. The third-order expansion accurately reproduces the projected transport operator and its associated pathway mixing. Beyond providing an efficient computational strategy, the transport operator reveals how coherent and dissipative perturbations redistribute amplitude among double-sided Feynman pathways, exposing mechanistic information that is not directly apparent from the nonlinear spectrum. SAKE thereby establishes a differentiable computational framework for nonlinear spectroscopy that supports efficient local parameter exploration, sensitivity analysis, and future inverse-design applications.

</details>


### [94] [Preparation of Large Fock States in Resonators with High Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20133)
*Lucas R. S. Santos,Ciro M. Diniz,Daniel Z. Rossatto,Celso J. Villas-Boas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合量子非破坏性（QND）光子数编码与量子振幅放大（QAA）的协议，实现了高概率预示性制备大Fock态。该方案从相干态开始的腔模出发，利用量子相位估计将光子数信息编码到多比特寄存器，并通过量子振幅放大提高目标结果的概率。在色散电路QED中可立即实现，最高可使用八比特寄存器近确定性制备数百激发的Fock态，并可扩展生成双模NOON态。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大Fock态是玻色子量子信息和量子增强计量学的重要资源，但在高激发数下高概率制备仍然困难：确定性方法需要日益复杂的控制，而基于测量的方法通常面临低预示概率问题。因此需要一种既能实现高成功概率又不需要过多控制开销的制备方案。

Method: 该协议结合两种关键技术：(1) 量子相位估计（QPE），从相干态腔模出发，将光子数信息非破坏性（QND）地编码到多比特寄存器中；(2) 量子振幅放大（QAA），在测量前提升目标输出结果的概率。方案在色散电路QED中可直接实现，也可类比推广到其他具有QND光子数读出的玻色子平台（如腔QED）。

Result: 使用最多八比特寄存器时，可实现近确定性地制备具有数百激发的Fock态。此外，该协议可作为条件分束器操作的第一阶段，进一步扩展生成双模NOON态。

Conclusion: 该方案提供了一条在大激发数下高概率制备Fock态的有效途径，兼具QND测量的非破坏性和振幅放大的概率提升优势，在现有电路QED平台上具有即时可行性，并为NOON态等更复杂量子态制备提供了铺垫。

Abstract: Large Fock states are important resources for bosonic quantum information and quantum-enhanced metrology, but preparing them with high probability at large excitation numbers remains challenging, as deterministic methods become increasingly control-intensive, while measurement-based approaches typically suffer from low heralding probabilities. Here we propose a protocol that combines quantum nondemolition photon-number encoding with quantum amplitude amplification to enable high-probability heralded generation of large Fock states. Starting from a cavity mode prepared in a coherent state, Quantum Phase Estimation encodes photon-number information into a multi-qubit register, while Quantum Amplitude Amplification boosts the probability of a desired target outcome before measurement. The scheme has an immediate implementation in dispersive circuit-QED, but can be analogously adapted to other bosonic platforms with QND photon-number readout, such as cavity-QED. With a register of up to eight qubits, near-deterministic preparation of Fock states with hundreds of excitations is possible. We also show that the protocol can serve as the first stage of an extension toward generating a two-mode NOON state via a conditional beam-splitter operation.

</details>


### [95] [Vacuum viscosity and relativistic inertia: Motion of a massive object with charged internal degrees of freedom interacting with a classical field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20140)
*Jen-Tsung Hsiang,Bei-Lok Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一个带电内部自由度的运动物体与自由量子场相互作用时是否会产生粘性力（真空粘滞）的问题。非相对论计算给出"是"的结论，但相对论协变计算给出"否"的答案，揭示出非相对论框架的缺陷，并在强制执行狭义相对论原理后，为牛顿第一、第二定律增加了丰富内容，同时给出了获得正确非相对论结果的规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究受两类自70年代以来研究的问题启发：一是宇宙学粒子产生及其更易处理的模拟——动力Casimir效应；二是中性原子沿介质表面运动时经历的量子摩擦。这些现象中粒子产生的反作用效应（如使宇宙膨胀各向同性化或减慢运动镜面）可以通过量子场涨落引起的真空粘滞来理解。作者希望追溯这一效应的起源，探究一个带电内部自由度的运动大质量物体与自由无界经典场在零温下相互作用时是否也会经历粘性力。

Method: 采用适用于光力学的微物理模型，该模型能够处理不等价的三体χ-φ-M相互作用。首先进行非相对论计算（考虑到原子物理需求，这看似完全合理），随后进行相对论协变计算，并对两者进行对比分析，定位非相对论框架的缺陷所在。

Result: 非相对论计算得出存在粘性力的肯定答案，但相对论协变计算给出否定答案。作者识别出非相对论框架中的缺陷位置，发现这一潜在但真实的矛盾在技术上解决起来不平凡。

Conclusion: 解决这一矛盾的物理洞见是，当强制执行狭义相对论原理时，牛顿第一和第二定律被赋予更丰富的内容，同时提供了获得正确非相对论答案所必须遵循的规则。这一结果在物理上极具启发性。

Abstract: Our present investigation into a rather rudimentary problem is motivated by two classes of problems studied since the 70's, cosmological particle creation and its more accessible analog, the dynamical Casimir effect on the one hand, and quantum friction a neutral atom moving along a dielectric surface would experience, on the other. The backreaction effects of produced particles being able to isotropize the expansion of the universe, or to slow down the moving mirror can be understood via the concept of vacuum viscosity arising from fluctuations of the quantum field. We want to track down the origin of this effect by asking the question whether a moving massive $M$ object with a charged internal degrees of freedom $χ$ interacting with a free unbounded classical field $φ$ at zero temperature would experience a viscous force, similar to the said precedents. Adopting a microphysics model for optomechanics which can treat the unequal tripartite $χ$-$φ$-$M$ interactions, we first perform a nonrelativistic calculation, which seems perfectly legitimate considering the needs of atomic physics, and found the answer to be yes, but a relativistic covariant calculation says no. We identify where the nonrelativistic framework is defective. The resolution of this latent yet real conflict is technically nontrivial but physically quite inspirational. It results in added enriched contents to Newton's first and second laws when the principles of special relativity are enforced, and rules to follow to get the correct nonrelativistic answer

</details>


### [96] [Proper Learning of Shallow All-to-All Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20162)
*Steven Kordonowy,Jacob Watkins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究浅层量子电路的学习问题，提出一个基于前端和后端迭代局部门逆变的元算法框架，用于学习随机全连接两局域量子电路。通过光锥增长分析，指出该电路系综在深度 d* ~ log₂n + log₂log₂n 处发生尖锐的可学习性转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子电路学习研究多聚焦于特定电路结构（如Haar随机砖墙电路），缺乏对不同类别电路的通用学习框架。同时，理解量子电路学习的难易程度对量子密码学方案及量子计算验证具有重要意义。

Method: 提出一种元算法框架：在电路前端和后端进行迭代的局部门逆变，逐步学习并重构电路中的具体量子门，生成操作等价电路。将该框架应用于随机全连接两局域电路，并结合光锥增长分析推导可学习性转变的深度阈值。

Result: 解析与数值证据表明，随机全连接两局域电路在深度 d* ~ log₂n + log₂log₂n（n为大小）处经历尖锐的可学习性转变：高于该深度电路难以学习，低于则可通过所提框架有效学习。

Conclusion: 该学习框架对量子电路学习提供了普适方法，其可学习性转变结果对依赖电路学习难度的量子密码方案有重要启示，但学习设置上的差异指出现有加密方案需进一步审视，并为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: This work considers a variation on the problem of learning shallow quantum circuits. Given query access to the circuit, as well as knowledge of its gate layout, we consider the task of learning the specific gates used in the circuit, producing an operationally-equivalent circuit matching this structure. Building on recent work for learning Haar random brickwork circuits, we identify a meta-algorithmic framework for learning broad classes of circuits based on iterative local gate inversions at the front and back of the circuit. We apply these techniques to study random, all-to-all, two-local circuits, and provide analytical and numerical evidence that this ensemble undergoes a sharp learnability transition at depth $d^* \sim \log_2 n + \log_2\log_2 n$ in the large size limit, based on an analysis of lightcone growth. These results have implications for recently proposed quantum cryptographic schemes based on the difficulty of circuit learning, though there are important distinctions with respect to our setting that suggest avenues for future study.

</details>


### [97] [Architecture and Compilation Co-Design for High-Rate Quantum Product Codes on Neutral Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20164)
*Adrian Liu,Wan-Hsuan Lin,Daniel Bochen Tan,Qian Xu,Jason Cong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出ONEX框架，针对中性原子阵列上的量子LDPC码执行计划（QEC编译）难题，将复杂的2D物理执行规划分解为独立的1D子问题，利用SMT编码获得深度最优解，并通过多阶段编译流水线实现实用编译时间。在HGP码存储器上，相比构造性1D算法和通用2D编译器分别提升3.7x-6.1x和29.8x-42.1x的时钟速率，可扩展到2500数据量子比特，并扩展到分区布局和lifted-product码族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要高编码率的QEC码，qLDPC码是理想候选，但合成有效且高效的物理执行计划是已被证明为困难的组合问题，构成关键编译瓶颈，且随码尺寸增长而恶化。需要一种能克服此复杂性、在实用编译时间内生成高质量执行计划的框架。

Method: ONEX框架的核心思想是利用qLDPC码族中具有维度缩减特性的乘积结构（如HGP码），将2D物理执行规划分解为两个独立的1D子问题。具体包括：1）将1D执行计划形式化为显式的SMT（可满足性模理论）编码，生成可证明深度最优的解；2）构建多阶段编译流水线，包含任意时间优化、运动压缩和迭代反馈，维持实用墙钟时间的同时提供渐进优化和按需检索；3）将框架扩展到分区布局和更广泛的lifted-product码族。

Result: 在映射到中性原子阵列的HGP码存储器应用中，ONEX相比构造性1D算法和通用2D编译器，分别实现3.7x-6.1x和29.8x-42.1x的时钟速率提升，且可高效扩展到2500数据量子比特的码。扩展到分区布局后揭示了相关架构权衡，并通过代表性示例展示了在LP码族上的适用性。

Conclusion: ONEX通过将2D执行规划分解为可独立求解的1D子问题，有效突破了qLDPC码编译的复杂性瓶颈，在保证深度最优性的同时实现了显著的时钟速率提升和接近实用的编译时间，为中性原子平台上大规模qLDPC码的实用容错量子计算铺平了道路。

Abstract: Achieving fault-tolerant quantum computing at a practical scale demands quantum error correction (QEC) codes with high encoding rates. Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes emerge as a promising candidate, especially given the rise of neutral atom arrays that provide dynamic long-range connectivity via atom movements. In general, synthesizing valid and efficient physical execution plans for QEC is a provably hard combinatorial problem, forming a critical compilation bottleneck that worsens as code sizes grow. To overcome this complexity, we focus on an important product family of qLDPC codes with dimension-reduction properties, and propose ONEX. This framework decomposes complex 2D physical execution planning into independent 1D subproblems, each solved to optimal execution depth within practical compilation time. First, we formulate the 1D execution plan with an explicit satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) encoding. This protocol produces provably depth-optimal solutions with substantial duration reduction. Second, we develop a multi-stage compilation pipeline featuring anytime optimization, movement compaction, and iterative feedback. This pipeline maintains practical wall-clock times while providing progressive refinement and on-demand retrieval of quality solutions. Third, we evaluate ONEX in the application of hypergraph product (HGP) code memory mapped onto neutral atom arrays, achieving 3.7x to 6.1x and 29.8x to 42.1x higher clock rates than the constructive 1D algorithm and the general 2D compiler, respectively, while scaling efficiently to codes with 2,500 data qubits. Finally, we extend ONEX to zoned layouts, revealing architectural insights into the associated trade-offs, and demonstrate its applicability to the broader lifted-product (LP) code family through a representative example.

</details>


### [98] [Large Scale Entanglement Structure Detection in 100-Qubit Systems via Local Joint Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20170)
*Rui Li,Yuhang Wang,Chunxiao Du,Shikun Zhang,Zheng Qin,Wenxiu Li,Hao Zhang,Zhisong Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的量子多体纠缠结构识别框架，通过选择满足边界匹配条件的局部泡利基，仅需单一测量配置即可从局部关联指纹中识别大尺度纠缠结构，测量开销与系统尺寸无关。在含噪声模拟中，该协议对多达100量子比特的系统能以超过95%的平均准确率分类GHZ、W和团簇型结构；并在超导量子处理器上对多达13量子比特的系统进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别多体量子态的纠缠结构（即其组成部分如何划分为非纠缠块）是量子信息科学的核心任务，但传统层析方法随系统规模呈指数级增长，难以适用于大尺度系统。

Method: 提出一种可扩展框架，直接从局部关联指纹中识别大尺度纠缠结构。关键创新在于选择满足边界匹配条件p_1 = p_R的代表性局部泡利基，使得整个链能在单一测量配置下读出全部信息，从而保持测量开销与系统大小无关。该方法在含噪声模拟和超导量子处理器上均进行了验证。

Result: 在含噪声模拟中，该单一基协议在30个候选分区间分类GHZ、W和团簇型结构，对多达100量子比特的系统平均准确率超过95%。在超导量子处理器上，能可靠分类至多达13量子比特的块结构，超过该规模后由于噪声和深度引起的退化而失效。

Conclusion: 通过明确映射这些失效模式，本文刻画了硬件级可扩展性的边界，并为在近期待实现的量子设备上表征纠缠结构指明了具体策略。

Abstract: Identifying the entanglement structure of a many-body quantum state, namely how its constituents partition into unentangled blocks, is a central task in quantum information science, yet conventional tomography scales exponentially with system size. Here we introduce a scalable framework that recognizes large-scale entanglement structures directly from local correlation fingerprints. By choosing a representative local Pauli basis that satisfies a boundary-matching condition p_1 = p_R, the entire chain is read out in a single measurement configuration, keeping the measurement effort independent of system size. In noisy simulations, this single-basis protocol classifies GHZ-, W-, and cluster-type structures among 30 candidate partitions with a mean accuracy exceeding 95% for systems of up to 100 qubits. We further validate the protocol on a superconducting quantum processor, where it reliably classifies block structures for systems of up to 13 qubits before noise- and depth-induced degradation sets in at larger sizes. By mapping these failure modes explicitly, our results delineate the boundary of hardware-level scalability and point to a concrete strategy for characterizing entanglement structure on near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [99] [Characterization of a damping channel as a mixture of amplitude damping and anti-damping channels of different parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20178)
*Vijay Pathak,R. Srikanth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过混合不等衰减参数的幅度阻尼和反阻尼通道，并引入时间依赖的混合概率来构造更广泛类别的相位协变动力学（phase-covariant dynamics）的框架。该框架能够独立控制收缩和平移，在非幺正（non-unital）和幺正（unital）区域之间连续调节，并产生广义振幅阻尼（GAD）通道所缺乏的有效退相干贡献。通过利用P-可分割性定理并在合适的希尔伯特空间基下进行评估，作者简化了幺正与非幺正情形下的条件，并展示了该框架可覆盖CP-可分割、P-可分割但非CP-可分割、以及非P-可分割等多种动力学行为。此外，适当的混合可减小与理想无噪声演化的偏差，且该改善在调节至幺正区域后仍然保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统中的非幺正噪声（如能量交换引起的布洛赫球态平移）是基本特征，但量子信息处理（尤其是纠错）往往偏好无平移的幺正动力学。现有的相位协变动力学模型如广义振幅阻尼（GAD）通道对耗散、激发和退相干过程的控制能力有限，缺乏独立调控收缩与平移、连续调节幺正/非幺正区域的灵活性。因此需要一种更通用的构造框架来工程化所需的开放系统动力学。

Method: 作者提出通过混合具有不等衰减参数的幅度阻尼通道（amplitude-damping）和反阻尼通道（anti-damping），并采用时间依赖的混合概率，构造更广泛的相位协变动力学。该方法实现：1) 独立控制收缩（contraction）和平移（translation）；2) 在非幺正与幺正区域间连续调谐；3) 产生GAD中缺失的有效退相干贡献。在表征方面，利用P-可分割性的一般定理，并在适当选择的希尔伯特空间基下评估，化简了幺正与非幺正情形的可分割性条件。

Result: 该框架能够覆盖多种动力学行为，包括CP-可分割、P-可分割但非CP-可分割、以及非P-可分割动力学。进一步研究表明，适当的通道混合可以减小与理想无噪声演化的偏差，且这种改善即使在动力学被调谐至幺正区域后仍然保持。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种超越标准热模型的灵活开放系统动力学工程设计方法，在噪声控制和量子信息处理方面具有潜在应用价值。其核心贡献在于统一并推广了相位协变动力学的构造框架，使其具备更强的可调性和更丰富的动力学行为谱。

Abstract: Non-unital noise is a fundamental feature of open quantum systems, governing energy exchange and inducing state translations on the Bloch sphere. While such translations can be useful for tasks such as state preparation and channel capacity, unital dynamics where no translation occurs are often preferred in quantum information processing, particularly for error correction. Phase-covariant dynamics provide a general framework encompassing dissipation, excitation, and dephasing processes in qubit systems; however, commonly used models such as the generalized amplitude damping (GAD) channel offer only limited control over these features. In this work, we present a constructive framework for generating a broader class of phase-covariant dynamics by mixing amplitude-damping and anti-damping channels with unequal decay parameters and time-dependent mixing probabilities. This approach enables independent control over contraction and translation, allows continuous tuning between non-unital and unital regimes, and yields an effective dephasing contribution absent in GAD. To characterize these dynamics, we employ the general theorem for P-divisibility and show that it can be used constructively by evaluating it in suitably chosen Hilbert space bases, leading to simplified conditions for both unital and non-unital cases. The framework captures a wide range of behaviors, including CP-divisible, P-divisible but not CP-divisible, and non-P-divisible dynamics. We further show that appropriate mixing can reduce the deviation from the ideal noiseless evolution and that such improvement persists even after tuning the dynamics to the unital regime. Our results provide a flexible approach to engineering open system dynamics beyond standard thermal models, with potential applications in noise control and quantum information processing.

</details>


### [100] [Hybrid Qubit-Rotor Quantum Systems: Clifford Structure, Universal Control, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20227)
*Dengyao Luo,Arvin Kushwaha,Mastawal Tirfe,Bojko N. Bakalov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为"n个量子比特 + r个量子转子"的混合量子寄存器建立了完整的Clifford理论与普适控制框架：分类了保持Weyl对易关系的混合相空间所有自同构，给出了每个自同构的有限Clifford电路；证明了转子动量奇偶性可控制量子比特Pauli操作（属于Clifford群），而任何非零的量子比特受控转子动量平移都是非Clifford的；添加转子余弦势和一个固定的量子比特-转子条件相位即可在强算子拓扑下实现全域普适控制。并将该框架应用于规范协变物质跳跃的精确受控位移实现、具有直接角度读数的转子相位估计、以及转子动量码上的有限傅里叶变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: U(1)量子转子（周期性角度与整数共轭动量的配对）出现在分子转动、超导相位-电荷电路和紧致规范场中。将这样的转子与量子比特相干耦合可构成混合寄存器，但其控制结构既不能从振子-量子比特情况也不能从qudit情况直接继承，需要有独立的Clifford理论与普适控制理论。

Method: (1) 对混合相空间 F_2^{2n} × Z^r × T^r 中保持Weyl对易关系的所有自同构进行分类，并为每种自同构给出显式的有限Clifford电路；(2) 建立混合耦合的规范形式，并给出合成所需基本混合门的最小数目；(3) 证明加入转子余弦势与一个固定的量子比特-转子条件相位后，结合局域Clifford操作可在强算子拓扑下实现普适控制；(4) 应用于三个具体场景：规范协变物质跳跃的精确受控位移（非Clifford）、转子相位估计、以及转子动量码的有限傅里叶变换（d=2^s 的单转子变换编译为 O(s) 个动量选择和受控位移指令，每个横跨寄存器的傅里叶因子由一个二次转子Clifford门实现）。

Result: 实现了混合量子比特-转子寄存器Clifford群的完整分类与显式合成电路；明确了Clifford边界（转子动量奇偶性控制Pauli属于Clifford，而受控转子动量平移非Clifford）；给出了混合耦合的规范形式和最小门的精确数量；证明了在强算子拓扑下的普适控制；并在三个应用场景中展示了该框架的实用效能。

Conclusion: 混合"量子比特+量子转子"寄存器具有独立于振荡器-qubit和qudit结构的新颖控制性质，通过建立完整的Clifford分类与普适控制定理，为该类系统提供了系统性的理论基础，并在规范场模拟、相位估计和傅里叶变换等问题上展现出直接而高效的应用价值。

Abstract: A $U(1)$ quantum rotor pairs a periodic angle with an integer-valued conjugate momentum, and occurs in molecular rotation, superconducting phase-charge circuits, and compact gauge fields. Coupling such a rotor coherently to qubits gives a hybrid register whose control structure is not inherited from either the oscillator-qubit or the qudit case. We develop a Clifford theory, together with a universal-control result, for registers of $n$ qubits and $r$ rotors. We classify all automorphisms of the hybrid phase space $\mathbb{F}_2^{2n}\times\mathbb{Z}^r\times\mathbb{T}^r$ that preserve the Weyl commutation relations, and give an explicit finite Clifford circuit for each one. The classification is directional: rotor momentum parity may control qubit Pauli operations within the Clifford group, while every nonzero qubit-controlled rotor momentum shift is non-Clifford. It also yields normal forms for the mixed qubit-rotor couplings and the exact minimum number of elementary mixed gates needed to synthesize them. Adding a rotor cosine potential and one fixed qubit-rotor conditional phase to the local Clifford operations gives universal control on the full Hilbert space in the strong operator topology. We then apply this structure in three settings: an exact controlled-shift realization of gauge-covariant matter hopping, which is necessarily non-Clifford; rotor phase estimation with direct angle readout and probe optimization under momentum-support and energy constraints; and finite Fourier transforms on rotor momentum codes, where the one-rotor transform for $d=2^s$ compiles into $O(s)$ momentum-selective and controlled-shift instructions and each cross-register Fourier factor is implemented by one quadratic rotor Clifford gate.

</details>


### [101] [Jamming Extensions of Quantum Correlations Lead to Hidden Superluminal Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20247)
*Ravishankar Ramanathan,Xie Sicheng,Michał Eckstein,Paweł Horodecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了与相对论因果性（RC）相容的关联集合，证明当底层态为量子态时，任何非平凡的jamming（无论是与态无关还是与态相关）必然导致隐藏的超光速信号传递。因此只有两种一致的可能性：无非堵塞的标准量子关联，或完整的相对论因果关联集合。作为应用，作者构造了能够攻破基于量子力学设备无关（DI）比特承诺和秘密共享协议的RC攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论因果性在某些时空中允许一种称为jamming的现象——即超光速的因果影响但不构成超光速信号传递。需要明确刻画哪些关联集合与相对论因果性相容，以及这种约束对设备无关密码学协议安全性的影响。

Method: 扩展PRL 104, 140404中的算子框架，对与相对论因果性相容的关联集合进行完全刻画。在此基础上，当底层量子态被约束时，证明非平凡的jamming必然导致隐藏的超光速信号传递。随后利用该刻画构造针对设备无关比特承诺和秘密共享协议的显式RC攻击。

Result: 证明了量子关联的中间jamming扩展必然导致信号传递，仅允许标准量子关联或完整的相对论因果关联集合。构造了在jamming几何中能攻破DI比特承诺和DI秘密共享的显式RC攻击，即使这些协议在no-signaling对手下是安全的。

Conclusion: 针对相对论对手的安全性要求行为的时序-空间位置信息必须与输入输出统计一起指定；仅凭输入输出统计数据不足以保证安全。这揭示了设备无关密码学中相对论因果约束下的安全假设需要重新审视。

Abstract: Relativistic causality in certain spacetime configurations permits jamming - superluminal causal influences that nevertheless do not enable superluminal signaling. We fully characterize the correlations compatible with relativistic causality (RC) by extending the operator framework of PRL 104, 140404. When the underlying state is required to be quantum, we prove that any nontrivial jamming - whether state-independent or state-dependent - necessarily leads to hidden superluminal signaling. Thus, the only consistent possibilities are standard quantum correlations without jamming or the full relativistically causal correlation set, with intermediate jamming extensions of quantum correlations leading to signaling. As an application of our characterization, we investigate the security of device-independent (DI) cryptographic primitives against adversaries constrained only by relativistic causality. We construct explicit RC attacks that break DI bit commitment and DI secret sharing in jamming geometries, even when these protocols remain secure against no-signaling adversaries. Our results show that security against relativistic adversaries requires behaviors to be specified together with the spacetime locations of their measurement events; input-output statistics alone are insufficient.

</details>


### [102] [Logarithmic depth compression of Heisenberg Hamiltonian simulation by fan-out parallelization, with built-in error detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20250)
*Artemiy Burov,Clément Javerzac*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于fan-out的gadget编译器，通过在Heisenberg型NMR哈密顿量模拟中将每个逻辑自旋编码为按交互度大小缩放的小型重复码寄存器，以增加电路宽度为代价换取电路深度的降低，并在NISQ硬件上实现了深度与体积的显著优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ量子计算机受限于电路深度，而自旋系统的乘积公式（product-formula）模拟会导致窄而深的电路，限制了可模拟系统的规模和保真度，亟需在不增加算法开销的前提下降低电路深度。

Method: 引入fan-out-based gadget编译器，将每个逻辑自旋编码为按交互度大小决定尺寸的小型重复码寄存器，使得所有同类型Pauli相互作用通过对数深度CNOT fan-out并行执行，冗余寄存器提供后选择的错误检测且无额外算法开销。在heavy-hex超导和全连接离子阱两种目标平台上进行固定协议的资源对比。

Result: 对于具有高度中心节点的交互图，体积最优调度将双量子比特深度减半，13自旋演示的电路体积降低1.7倍；在heavy-hex上双量子比特门数也降低，在全连接平台上最高度分子的深度降低达2.5倍。全连接平台上所有系统的双量子比特门数均上升，因此体积降低只对深度受限硬件有益。增益随交互图度不均匀性增加而增大，在稠密均匀图中消失。在基于当前处理器校准的噪声模型下，只有结合内置错误检测的后选择，gadget电路才能在误差率改善一个到一半数量级后匹配或超越顺序编译。

Conclusion: 该fan-out-based gadget编译器在深度受限的NISQ硬件上对具有度不均匀性交互图的NMR模拟具有显著优势，其内置错误检测与后选择结合是达到或超越顺序编译的必要条件，并已通过独立经典计算验证了光谱结果。

Abstract: Noisy intermediate-scale quantum computers are constrained by circuit depth, while product-formula simulation of spin systems leads to narrow and deep circuits. Here we introduce a fan-out-based gadget compiler that trades circuit depth for width in simulations of Heisenberg-type nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) Hamiltonians. Each logical spin is encoded into a small repetition-code register sized by its interaction degree, so that all pairwise interactions of a given Pauli type execute in parallel after a logarithmic-depth CNOT fan-out, and the redundant registers provide error detection for post-selection at no additional algorithmic overhead. The central result is a fixed-protocol resource comparison of the two compilations, transpiled to heavy-hex superconducting and all-to-all trapped-ion targets across a set of NMR spin systems. For interaction graphs with a high-degree hub the volume-optimal schedule halves the two-qubit depth and reduces the volume 1.7-fold for the 13-spin demonstration, which on heavy-hex also lowers the two-qubit gate count, and the depth reduction rises to 2.5-fold on all-to-all for the highest-degree molecule studied. On all-to-all the two-qubit gate count rises for every system, so the volume reduction is a benefit on depth-limited hardware. The gain grows with the degree inhomogeneity of the interaction graph and vanishes for dense uniform graphs, where the optimum is the sequential circuit. We simulate the zero-field NMR spectrum of tetramethylsilane, a 13-spin star system. Under a noise model scaled from a published present-day processor calibration, the shallower gadget circuits match or surpass the sequential compilation only after post-selection on their built-in error detection, once error rates improve by one to one and a half orders of magnitude. We verify the spectra against an independent classical computation.

</details>


### [103] [Analytical Solutions of the Generalized Klein-Gordon Oscillator in Som-Raychaudhuri Space-Time via the Extended Nikiforov-Uvarov Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20273)
*Hale Karayer,Tolga Celik,Dogan Demirhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用推广的Nikiforov-Uvarov方法，在Som-Raychaudhuri时空背景下，解析求解了均匀磁场中广义Klein-Gordon谐振子的精确本征态解，分别考虑了线性势和Cornell势两种模型，给出了能量本征值并图示分析了相应的本征函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空（Som-Raychaudhuri时空）中研究相对论性量子系统的精确解具有重要意义，有助于深入理解引力场与量子系统之间的相互作用。同时，推广的Nikiforov-Uvarov方法为求解这类二阶微分方程提供了一条有效的解析路径。

Method: 采用推广的Nikiforov-Uvarov (NU) 方法，在Som-Raychaudhuri时空背景下，构造均匀磁场中广义Klein-Gordon谐振子的波动方程，并针对线性势和Cornell势两种势模型分别进行解析求解，得到精确的能量本征值表达式和本征函数。

Result: 获得了线性势和Cornell势两种模型下广义Klein-Gordon谐振子的精确本征态解，给出了对应的能量本征值，并通过图形方式分析和展示了相关本征函数的物理行为。

Conclusion: 推广的Nikiforov-Uvarov方法能够有效地求解Som-Raychaudhuri时空中磁场作用下Klein-Gordon谐振子的精确解，为该类弯曲时空相对论量子系统的研究提供了新的分析工具和理论结果。

Abstract: In this study, we present an analytical approach based on the extended Nikiforov-Uvarov method to solve the generalized Klein-Gordon oscillator in the presence of a uniform magnetic field within the Som-Raychaudhuri space-time. Exact eigenstate solutions are obtained for two distinct potential models, namely the linear potential and the Cornell potential. The corresponding energy eigenvalues are determined, and the associated eigenfunctions are analyzed and illustrated graphically.

</details>


### [104] [Parallel Quantum Advantage with Limited Adaptivity Requires Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20297)
*Qipeng Liu,Saachi Mutreja*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了并行量子查询算法的几乎处处经典模拟定理，对Aaronson和Ambainis的猜想取得了进展。作者通过证明并行量子查询算法无法区分均匀随机预言机与"稠密分布"预言机，建立了模拟定理，并将其扩展到带前缀和混合自适应查询的算法，最终推广到常数轮适应性的量子算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson和Ambainis猜想量子查询算法存在高效的几乎处处经典模拟，即任何T查询量子算法都可以用多项式经典查询在(1-δ)比例输入上近似其接受概率。该猜想若成立，说明指数级量子加速仅可能出现在具有充分结构化的输入上。这一猜想的证明对理解量子计算的优势本质具有重要意义。

Method: 本文的核心技术贡献是提出一个耦合定理，将均匀分布预言机与从"稠密分布"中抽取的预言机联系起来。基于此耦合定理，作者证明了并行量子查询算法无法区分这两种分布，从而建立模拟定理。随后将该方法推广到三种更一般的场景：有界量子查询前缀后接大规模并行量子查询的算法、任意多项式数量自适应经典查询后接大规模并行量子查询的混合算法，以及以并行模拟定理为基元、推广到常数轮自适应性的量子算法。

Result: 成功证明了并行量子查询算法的几乎处处经典模拟定理，并进一步将其扩展到带量子前缀或混合经典查询的场景，以及常数轮适应性的量子算法。值得注意的是，Yamakawa和Zhandry此前的工作表明并行量子查询对采样问题仍可相对经典算法获得指数加速，但本文的模拟定理说明对于判定问题，并行量子查询的优势可以被经典模拟。

Conclusion: 本文对Aaronson-Ambainis猜想取得了实质性进展，证明了大规模并行量子查询算法的模拟定理，并通过耦合定理这一关键技术建立了方法论基础。该工作为最终解决更一般情况下的模拟猜想提供了重要的理论工具和范式。

Abstract: Aaronson and Ambainis (Theory of Computing, 2014) conjectured that quantum query algorithms admit efficient almost-everywhere classical simulation: for any $T$-query quantum algorithm, its acceptance probability can be approximated on a $(1-δ)$ fraction of inputs, up to $ε$ additive error, using $\mathrm{poly}(T, 1/ε, 1/δ)$ classical queries. At a high level, the conjecture suggests that exponential quantum speedups are possible only on sufficiently structured inputs.
  In this work, we make progress on this conjecture by proving it for quantum algorithms that make massively parallel quantum queries. In contrast, Yamakawa and Zhandry (Journal of the ACM, 2024) showed that quantum algorithms restricted to parallel queries can still achieve exponential speedups over classical algorithms for sampling problems. We establish our simulation theorem by proving the stronger statement that parallel-query quantum algorithms cannot distinguish the uniform distribution over oracles from oracles drawn from so-called "dense distributions". Our main technical contribution is a coupling theorem that relates the uniform distribution over oracles to oracles drawn from dense distributions.
  We further extend this approach beyond the purely parallel setting, obtaining simulation theorems both for algorithms with a bounded quantum-query prefix followed by a massively parallel quantum-query stage, and for hybrid algorithms that make an arbitrary polynomial number of adaptive classical queries before the massively parallel quantum-query stage. Finally, using the parallel-query simulation theorem as a base case, we obtain simulation theorems for quantum algorithms with constant rounds of adaptivity.

</details>


### [105] [Group-theoretic treatment of strong light-matter coupling with an arbitrary number of excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20340)
*Antti Peltola,Olli Siltanen,Kimmo Luoma,Konstantinos S. Daskalakis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用量子发射体的组合结构，研究了具有任意激发数的Tavis-Cummings模型，推导了其本征系统的结构和性质，识别了实际规模系统中允许的辐射跃迁，揭示了少数激发区域无法接触的新行为，并提供了降低具有指数增长Hilbert空间系统计算复杂度的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学微腔中的强光-物质相互作用产生极化激元，是现代技术中活跃使用的对象，但理论描述通常局限于单激发情形，难以捕捉多激发物理，阻碍了技术的进一步发展。作者旨在突破这一限制，研究任意激发数下的Tavis-Cummings模型。

Method: 利用量子发射体的组合结构，推导Tavis-Cummings模型在任意激发数下的本征系统结构与性质，并识别实际规模系统中允许的辐射跃迁。

Result: 揭示了少数激发区域无法接触的新行为，并提供了一个降低具有指数增长Hilbert空间系统计算复杂度的框架。

Conclusion: 通过组合结构分析，本工作扩展了对Tavis-Cummings模型多激发物理的理解，为实际规模量子光学系统的研究和计算提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Strong light-matter interactions in optical microcavities give rise to hybrid light-matter states known as polaritons. While actively used in modern technologies, theoretical descriptions of such systems are often restricted to the single-excitation case, limiting their ability to capture many-excitation physics and hindering further technological advancements. Here, by exploiting the combinatorial structure of quantum emitters, we investigate the Tavis-Cummings model with arbitrary number of excitations. We derive the structure and properties of its eigensystem and identify allowed radiative transitions in systems of realistic size scales. Our work reveals new behavior inaccessible to the few-excitation regime, while also providing a framework to reduce the computational complexity of similar systems with exponentially growing Hilbert spaces.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

### [106] [Exact expressions of correlation functions between two spins in the boundary row of the two-dimensional rectangular Ising model with periodic-free boundary conditions and finite size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19274)
*Mei Tao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文给出了二维矩形Ising模型在周期-自由边界条件下，边界行两自旋关联函数的精确表达式，并证明这些表达式在热力学极限下与已有研究结果形式一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算有限尺寸二维Ising模型边界行的自旋关联函数，并验证其在热力学极限下与已有结果的一致性，同时通过关联函数的精确表达式清晰地展示Ising模型长程有序随温度变化这一基本性质。

Method: 针对具有周期-自由边界条件的二维矩形Ising模型，推导出边界行两个自旋之间关联函数的精确表达式；通过分析参数L和N取极限顺序对热力学极限下关联函数表达式的影响，并借助图表展示不同尺寸对关联函数的影响；最终在热力学极限下与之前研究者的表达式进行形式比对并证明其一致性。

Result: 获得了有限尺寸二维矩形Ising模型边界行自旋关联函数的精确表达式；证明了在热力学极限下，本文所得表达式与先前研究者给出的表达式形式相同；通过图表阐明了不同系统尺寸对关联函数的影响。

Conclusion: 本文提供的精确关联函数表达式正确有效，在热力学极限下与已知结果一致，并清晰展示了Ising模型中长程有序随温度降低而涌现的物理现象，以及两个参数L和N取极限顺序对结果的影响。

Abstract: We present exact expressions of correlation functions between two spins in the boundary row of the two-dimensional rectangular Ising model with periodic-free boundary conditions and finite size. Some properties of exact expressions obtained are discussed. The fundamental property of the Ising model that the long range order emerge as the temperature decrease is shown clearly; expressions of the correlation functions in the thermodynamic limit varies depending on the order of taking limits of two parameters L and N; the impact of different sizes on correlation functions is illustrated with the aid of diagrams. Expressions of the correlation function discussed in this paper in the thermodynamic limit has been presented by previous researchers, and we prove that expressions obtained in this article is identical in form to expressions provided by previous researchers in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum Rényi-Jarzynski Equality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19320)
*Benjamin Bobell,Mert Okyay,Rahul Nandkishore*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用资源理论方法推导了基于任意浴观测量结果的无损量子Jarzynski等式，即Rényi-Jarzynski等式，该等式通过Rényi k-散度量化有限浴在非绝热驱动下偏离平衡的程度，并为量子最优控制提供可调的成本函数，可用于最小化有限量子系统中的串扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型的量子Jarzynski等式依赖测量协议，而测量会破坏量子相干性。作者希望发展一种不破坏相干性的量子Jarzynski等式，从而能够更好地量化有限浴在非绝热驱动下的平衡漂移。

Method: 采用资源理论（resource-theoretic）方法推导无损量子Jarzynski等式，将其条件化为任意浴观测量结果；得到Rényi-Jarzynski等式，以Rényi k-散度量化浴的漂移；并将其应用于量子最优控制，作为可调成本函数来最小化浴漂移（如态制备和门设计中减少串扰）。

Result: Rényi-Jarzynski等式为量子最优控制提供了可调成本函数；玩具模型展示了对于某个临界k值，竞争最小值之间发生转变，说明Rényi阶数可调节对浴分布不同区域的敏感性；当驱动参数跨浴能级变化时，最小化浴漂移需要产生系统-浴纠缠。

Conclusion: 该工作通过资源理论路径建立了非破坏性的Jarzynski等式，并将其与量子最优控制联系起来，揭示了Rényi阶数作为敏感性调节参量的作用，以及系统-浴纠缠在最小化浴漂移中的必要性。

Abstract: The Jarzynski equality provides a strict link between nonequilibrium work and equilibrium free energy changes. Its typical quantum formulations, however, rely on measurement protocols that destroy coherence. In this Letter, we use the resource-theoretic approach to derive a non-destructive quantum Jarzynski equality conditioned on the outcomes of an arbitrary bath observable. This yields the Rényi-Jarzynski equality, which quantifies a finite bath's drift from equilibrium under a non-adiabatic drive via the Rényi $k$-divergence. We further demonstrate that the Rényi-Jarzynski equality provides a tunable cost function for quantum optimal control problems where minimizing bath drift is desired, such as state preparation and gate design, enabling the minimization of cross-talk in finite quantum systems. Our toy model exhibits a transition between competing minima for some critical value of $k$, illustrating how the Rényi order tunes sensitivity to different regions of a bath distribution. Strikingly, when drive parameters vary across bath energy levels, minimizing bath drift requires generating system-bath entanglement.

</details>


### [108] [State convertibility and fluctuation theorems from a dynamical reference: majorization meets martingales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19391)
*Davide Cugini,Giacomo Guarnieri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文统一并推广了态可转换性（state convertibility）的判据，提出g(t)-majorization概念，将其转化为一维凸序问题，证明转换成立当且仅当相对布居分布由鞅连接，并由此推导出参考相对熵产生的精确涨落定理，该定理的平均违反可通过χ²散度界限认证参考演化与实际演化的失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在热力学中，态转换是否可行取决于是否保持参考态（通常为微正则或正则形式），这对应majorization和thermo-majorization判据，需要构造和比较态依赖的Lorenz曲线。作者希望将这些判据统一并推广到任意（可能随时间变化的）参考分布g(t)下，同时提供一种无需独立表征参考演化的模型无关诊断方法。

Method: 首先将majorization类判据推广为g(t)-majorization，使之适用于任意且可能含时的参考分布；随后引入对偶图像，将态可转换性问题转化为一维凸序问题，从而证明转换可行当且仅当相对布居k_j(t)/g_j(t)所构成的实值分布之间存在鞅关系；在此基础上推导参考相对熵产生的精确涨落定理，并用χ²-散度界将观测到的涨落关系破坏转化为参考误差的认证下限。

Result: 建立了g(t)-majorization判据及其等价的一维鞅条件；导出了参考相对熵产生的精确涨落定理；证明了该涨落关系的平均违反与χ²-散度下界之间的定量联系，能够为假定参考演化与真实参考演化之间的失配提供可认证的下界估计，且无需对真实参考演化进行独立表征。

Conclusion: 该工作为任意参考分布下的态可转换性提供了统一框架，同时将涨落定理的破坏转化为对参考模型误差的定量诊断工具，具有模型无关性和广泛适用性。

Abstract: State convertibility represents a fundamental concept used to determine whether a transformation is possible given a specific set of resources. Within the field of Thermodynamics, where physical process are required to preserve a reference state typically in microcanonical or canonical form, this translates into the notions of majorization and thermo-majorization ---criteria that require constructing and comparing state-dependent Lorenz curves. In this work, we firstly unify and extend these notions to an arbitrary and possibly time-dependent reference distribution $g(t)$, introducing the concept of $g(t)$-majorization; we then introduce a dual picture whereby state convertibility is turned into a one-dimensional convex-order problem, which allows us to demonstrate that a transition is admissible if and only if the associated real-valued distributions of relative populations $ k_j(t)/g_j(t)$ are connected by a martingale. Building on it, we then derive an exact fluctuation theorem for a reference-relative entropy production whose average violation certifies, through a $χ^{2}$-divergence bound, the mismatch between an assumed and the true reference evolution---a model-independent diagnostic that requires no independent characterization of the latter and turns an observed breakdown of the fluctuation relation into a certified lower bound on the reference error.

</details>


### [109] [Statistical complexity from fluctuations in the information content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19485)
*Renio S. Mendes,Sergio Picoli,Evaldo M. F. Curado*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出信息论量"信息含量方差"(C)可自然解释为统计复杂度度量，满足公认的统计复杂度标准（有序和等概率状态为零，中间态最大），并与热力学直接关联，在连续相变处达到峰值，可用于混沌映射和分形高斯噪声等不同相关系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计复杂度的量化一直是复杂系统研究的核心问题之一。现有统计复杂度测度往往缺乏与热力学和相变的直接联系，且在不同临界现象中的行为不够清晰。作者旨在寻找一个既满足统计复杂度基本标准、又能建立与物理机制（如能量涨落、热容）直接关联的度量。

Method: 作者论证了信息含量方差C作为统计复杂度测度的合理性：(1) 验证其满足复杂度测度的公认准则（有序态与等概率态为零，中间态最大）；(2) 推导C在玻尔兹曼-吉布斯统计下与能量涨落和热容成正比且具有广延性；(3) 通过二维Ising模型验证其在连续相变处达到最大值；(4) 应用于混沌映射和分数高斯噪声验证其捕捉不同相关系统动力学结构的能力。

Result: 结果表明C是一个满足统计复杂度所有公认标准的测度，且与热力学有直接数学联系（正比于热容和能量涨落），在二维Ising模型的连续相变点达到峰值，并能有效捕捉混沌映射和分数高斯噪声中的非平凡动力学结构。

Conclusion: 信息含量方差C提供了一种兼具理论严谨性和物理直觉的统计复杂度测度，其与热力学量的直接关联使其特别适用于相变和临界现象研究，优于其他无法在连续相变处达到最大值的复杂度测度。

Abstract: We argue that the variance of the information content ($C$), an information-theoretic quantity, can be naturally interpreted as a measure of statistical complexity. We show that $C$ satisfies widely accepted criteria for statistical complexity measures: it vanishes for both ordered and equiprobable states, while attaining maxima in intermediate regimes, typically shifted toward order. This interpretation establishes direct connections with thermodynamics and phase transitions: for systems obeying Boltzmann--Gibbs statistics, $C$ is extensive and directly proportional to energy fluctuations and heat capacity. Moreover, unlike other statistical complexity measures, it attains a maximum at continuous phase transitions, as illustrated for the two-dimensional Ising model. Applications to chaotic maps and fractional Gaussian noise further indicate that $C$ captures nontrivial dynamical structure in different classes of correlated systems.

</details>


### [110] [Learning Potts Models and $Z_3$ Toric Codes: Higher and Ordinary Nishimori Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20268)
*Rushikesh A. Patil,Malte Pütz,Rohit Mukherjee,Guo-Yi Zhu,Simon Trebst,Andreas W. W. Ludwig*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在二维q态Potts模型（2<q≤4）的键能测量学习相图中识别出更高阶的Nishimori线，该线与临界温度线交汇于一个三临界点（即更高Nishimori临界点），分离顺磁、铁磁和自旋玻璃相。作者用解析工具（高斯测量协议）和数值工具（离散q态测量协议）确定了丰富的相图结构、普适量（如Edwards-Anderson关联函数衰减指数）以及RG流，并讨论了Casimir有效中心荷沿测量诱导RG流的单调递减行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受先前Ising模型研究的启发，作者希望将更高Nishimori线及更高Nishimori临界点的概念推广到更高内部自由度的q态Potts模型（2<q≤4），以揭示其学习相图中更丰富的相结构，并为监控变形Z_q toric code中的信息临界点提供统一描述。

Method: （1）采用高斯测量协议实现精确解析计算，推导Edwards-Anderson关联函数衰减指数等普适量的精确结果；（2）采用通用离散q态测量协议进行大规模数值模拟，精确估计普通与更高Nishimori临界点位置及RG流；（3）利用c-effective定理及Elitzur定理进行非微扰论证，分析固定点稳定性及Casimir有效中心荷的单调性。

Result: 识别出更高Nishimori临界点（三临界点），相图含有两个不稳定和三个稳定固定点；确定了Edwards-Anderson关联函数衰减指数的精确值；给出了离散测量协议下临界点位置的精确数值估计；发现Casimir有效中心荷沿测量诱导RG流单调减小（与随机键Potts模型中单调增加形成对比）；通过Elitzur定理证明了普通Nishimori临界点的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了q态Potts模型学习相图中更高Nishimori线的完整图景，揭示了测量诱导RG流与随机键RG流之间中心荷演化方向的根本性差异，并将结果等价地映射为监控变形Z_q toric code中的信息临界点，统一了量子、经典及无记忆相之间的过渡。

Abstract: Motivated by a previous Ising study, we identify a ${\it higher}$ Nishimori line in the learning phase diagram of the $2D$ $q$-state Potts model $(2 < q\leq 4)$ under bond-energy measurements. This ${\it higher}$ Nishimori line meets the critical temperature line of the Potts model, in a ${\it higher}$ Nishimori critical point -- a tricritical point at finite inference strength that separates a paramagnetic, a ferromagnetic and a 'spin-glass' phase. With analytical tools, we discuss the general structure of the rich phase diagram, which contains two unstable and three stable fixed points, and obtain a number of exact results for universal quantities, including the decay exponent of the Edwards-Anderson correlator, using a Gaussian measurement protocol which allows for exact calculations. Using extensive numerical tools, we confirm these statements for a generic, discrete $q$-state measurement protocol and determine precise numerical estimates for the location of higher and ordinary Nishimori critical points as well as RG flows between the various fixed points. We also discuss the Casimir effective central charges of the critical points in the learning phase diagram, and their monotonic ${\it decrease}$ along measurement-induced RG flows, as established non-perturbatively by the c-effective theorem and its extensions, and contrast it to the monotonic increase along the corresponding RG flows in the random-bond Potts model. Finally, we discuss a general argument based on ${\it Elitzur's \; theorem}$ that establishes stability of the ordinary Nishimori critical points in their respective learning phase diagrams. Equivalently, our results describe a monitored deformed $\mathbb{Z}_q$ toric code where the tricritical ${\it higher}$ Nishimori point is an 'information' critical point that separates stable quantum, classical, and no memory phases.

</details>


### [111] [Exact partition function of arithmetic Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19605)
*Anu Dhochak,Ken Kikuchi,Shrinit Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文给出了d维算术Ising模型（AIM）配分函数的紧凑精确公式，并在2×2系统中利用q-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数解析表达了自由能和熵，发现高温下熵增，支持熵序的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算术Ising模型作为一种重要的统计物理模型，其精确配分函数的求解具有挑战性。作者希望获得该模型配分函数的紧凑解析形式，并通过自由能和熵的显式表达来理解其热力学性质，特别是高温度区域的行为。

Method: 本文采用解析方法，推导了d维算术Ising模型配分函数的紧凑公式。对于2×2系统，使用q-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数给出了配分函数的解析表达式，并由此推导出自由能和熵的显式形式。

Result: 获得了配分函数的紧凑表达式；在2×2系统中解析表达了自由能和熵；发现高温区域熵增加，这与熵序的存在一致。

Conclusion: 该结果不仅为算术Ising模型提供了精确的数学框架，还通过高温熵增现象支持了系统中存在熵序的结论，为理解这类模型的统计力学性质提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: We present a compact formula for the exact partition function of the $d$-dimensional arithmetic Ising model (AIM). For a $2\times2$ system, we express it analytically using the $q$-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta function and derive explicit forms for the free energy and entropy. Additionally, we find that the entropy increases at high temperatures, supporting the presence of entropic order.

</details>


### [112] [Exact Fluctuation-Response Relations for Underdamped Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20013)
*Tan Van Vu,Van Tuan Vo,Ruicheng Bao,Keiji Saito*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对欠阻尼动力学中传统平均电流热力学不确定关系失效的问题，推导出任意时间依赖驱动和一般加性观测量下欠阻尼Langevin动力学的精确有限时间涨落-响应等式，建立了响应与耗散之间的直接联系，并据此推出可饱和的摩擦-响应热力学不确定关系及总熵产生的精确变分原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠阻尼动力学中传统基于平均电流的热力学不确定关系可能失效，涨落、响应与耗散之间的深层联系仍不清楚，急需从理论上揭示这一结构。

Method: 推导精确的有限时间涨落-响应等式（适用于任意时间依赖驱动和一般加性可观测量），从该等式出发获得尖锐的响应界和动态生成方差的变分刻画；沿不可逆概率流选取微扰，得到摩擦-响应热力学不确定关系，并可在任意有限观测时间饱和，从而导出总熵产生的精确变分原理。

Result: 得到了可饱和的摩擦-响应热力学不确定关系；证明在驱动自由扩散中，传统速度分辨电流的不确定因子可随耗散指数衰减，而摩擦-响应因子保持其普适下界。这表明在欠阻尼动力学中，连接涨落与耗散的直接量是响应而非平均电流本身。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了欠阻尼动力学中响应作为涨落与耗散之间直接桥梁的理论框架，为有限时间热力学不确定关系提供了可饱和的普适刻画，深化了对非平衡涨落-响应-耗散关系的理解。

Abstract: Thermodynamic uncertainty relations connect current fluctuations to dissipation and are often rooted in fluctuation-response principles. In underdamped dynamics, however, conventional mean-current uncertainty relations can fail, while the underlying connection between fluctuations, response, and dissipation remains elusive. Here we uncover this structure by deriving an exact finite-time fluctuation-response equality for underdamped Langevin dynamics, valid for arbitrary time-dependent driving and general additive observables. The equality yields sharp response bounds and a variational characterization of the dynamically generated variance. Choosing the perturbation along the irreversible probability flow gives a friction-response thermodynamic uncertainty relation that can be saturated at any finite observation time, leading to an exact variational principle for the total entropy production. We further show that the conventional uncertainty factor of velocity-resolved currents can decay exponentially with dissipation even in driven free diffusion, while the friction-response factor retains its universal lower bound. These results establish response, rather than the mean current itself, as the quantity directly linking fluctuations and dissipation in underdamped dynamics.

</details>


### [113] [Fluctuation-Controlled Asymmetric Kinetics in Metal-Insulator Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20100)
*Tapas Bar,David Pesquera,Timm Swoboda,Arnau Villalobos-Martin,Cristian Rodriguez-Tinoco,Pol Lloveras,Marianna Sledzinska,Javier Rodriguez-Viejo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过输运、量热和拉曼测量揭示了1T-TaS₂中热驱动金属-绝缘体相变的非对称动力学：冷却时相变以爆发式雪崩进行（非热过程），而加热时则连续平滑转变（热涨落主导）。通过脉冲焦耳加热的非平衡热扰动研究证明两种滞后分支具有不同的非热性程度，从而决定了转变路径的差异，建立了非热性程度控制一级相变路径选择的一般框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一级相变中热激活与非热（雪崩式）转变之间的竞争机制，以及同一相变中不同热力学路径（冷却与加热）为何表现出截然不同的动力学行为。

Method: 结合宏观输运测量、量热测量和拉曼光谱作为局部探针；采用脉冲焦耳加热产生受控非平衡热扰动，用以区分冷却和加热过程中转变动力学的热性与非热性特征。

Result: 发现冷却分支呈现爆发式雪崩转变（非热行为），加热分支则连续平滑（热涨落克服成核势垒）；通过对热扰动的不同响应证实了两条滞后分支具有不同的非热性程度，揭示了体输运中由于逾渗导电被遮蔽的内在动力学不对称性。

Conclusion: 建立了"非热性程度决定一级相变路径选择"的一般框架，表明相变动力学的非热性程度（athermality）是控制冷却与加热路径不对称性的关键参数。

Abstract: We report asymmetric kinetics in thermally driven metal-insulator transitions (MITs) in 1T-TaS$_2$. Using combined transport, calorimetric, and Raman measurements, we show that the transition proceeds via burst-like avalanches during cooling, while remaining continuous during heating. Although bulk transport is masked by percolative conduction, local probes and thermal measurements reveal intrinsic asymmetry in the transformation pathways. Using controlled nonequilibrium thermal perturbations generated by pulsed Joule heating, we demonstrate that the phase-ordering dynamics remains strongly athermal during cooling, whereas during heating fluctuations progressively overcome nucleation barriers, leading to a smooth transformation. The distinct responses to thermal perturbations indicate different degrees of athermality of the two hysteresis branches, which govern the transformation pathways and give rise to the observed kinetic asymmetry. These results establish a general framework in which the degree of athermality controls pathway selection in first-order phase transitions.

</details>


### [114] [Biorthogonal Conformal Dynamics in Non-Hermitian Quantum Quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20156)
*Yifan Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文建立了相互作用非厄米临界系统中双正交全局淬火的边界共形场论（BCFT）框架。通过独立左/右制备流同到同一共形边界，定义具有复外推参数的条带几何，并在线性/反线性对称配对约束下，在相互作用Yang-Lee自旋链中精确预测了主单点函数的时间中心（相对误差10^-3量级）及局域相位演化等通用淬火后动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维临界点的全局淬火可借助边界共形场论描述，将欧几里得条带关联函数解析延拓至实时。然而现有框架针对厄米系统，在非厄米临界系统中，右ket单独无法确定动力学读出，左协矢是微观淬火协议的一部分，需要建立统一的双正交BCFT框架以描述这类系统的淬火后普适动力学。

Method: 将左右独立制备态流同到同一共形边界，定义条带两个时间边界并引入一般复数的单侧外推参数。利用线性（L-paired）和反线性（A-paired）对称配对约束几何。在相互作用Yang-Lee自旋链中，通过静态校准的边界数据计算双正交的完整特征返回振幅、主单点函数和空间关联函数，并通过解析延拓路径分析边界块。

Result: 复线性配对外推参数的虚部以10^-3相对精度预测了独立演化的反线性配对单点函数的时间中心；同一制备相位控制局域相位演化和边界块解析延拓路径。此外通过直接加场淬火、混合左/右制备及复杂五态Potts不动点（含复主维数）进一步验证了该形式体系。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了相互作用非厄米临界系统中双正交全局淬火的BCFT框架，揭示了复时间边界数据如何组织普适的淬火后动力学，为理解非厄米临界系统的动力学普适性提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Global quenches at one-dimensional critical points admit a boundary conformal field theory (BCFT) description in which Euclidean strip correlators are analytically continued to real time. We formulate this construction for interacting non-Hermitian critical systems, where a right ket alone does not specify the dynamical readout and the left covector is part of the microscopic quench protocol. Independent left and right preparations flowing to the same conformal boundary define the two temporal boundaries of a strip with generally complex one-sided extrapolation parameters; linear and antilinear symmetry pairings are exploited to constrain this geometry. In the interacting Yang-Lee spin chain, statically calibrated boundary data determine the biorthogonal dynamics of the complete-character return amplitude, a primary one-point function, and a spatial correlator. The imaginary part of the linear-paired extrapolation parameter predicts the temporal center of an independently evolved antilinear-paired one-point function at the $10^{-3}$ relative level; the same preparation phase controls local phase evolution and the analytic-continuation path of boundary blocks. Further results test this formalism with a direct field-on quench, mixed left and right preparations, and a complex five-state Potts fixed point with complex primary dimensions. These results establish a BCFT framework for biorthogonal global quenches in interacting non-Hermitian critical systems, in which complex temporal-boundary data organize universal post-quench dynamics.

</details>


### [115] [Splitting probabilities for Brownian motion with diffusing boundaries: Application to polymer translocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20226)
*Alexander K. Hartmann,Satya N. Majumdar,Alberto Rosso*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究聚合物链通过纳米孔转运过程，其中链长因聚合-解聚过程而随机波动。将问题映射为孔隙在含两个扩散汇（sinks）的一维线上做随机游走的等效表示，精确计算了分裂概率、转运时间分布和完成时链长分布，并发现其幂律尾指数连续依赖于扩散常数。数值模拟验证了理论结果，并扩展到分数布朗运动情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物链通过纳米孔的转运是生物物理中的核心过程（如DNA测序、蛋白质易位）。传统理论大多假设链长固定（即汇固定不动），但实际体系中链两端会发生聚合-解聚反应，导致链长随时间随机涨落。作者旨在揭示移动汇（即链长波动）对转运动力学定性且定量上的显著影响。

Method: 将聚合物转运映射为一维随机游走问题：孔隙运动扩散常数为D2，左右两侧各有一个移动汇（对应聚合-解聚导致的链端边界），扩散常数分别为D1和D3。转运在孔隙碰到任一外缘汇时终止。作者精确求解了分裂概率（孔隙先碰到左侧或右侧汇的概率），以及转运时间的概率分布和完成时链长的概率分布。理论推导通过数值模拟验证；进一步对孔隙做分数布朗运动（Hurst指数0<H<1）的情形进行了数值研究。

Result: 发现移动汇情形下的分裂概率与经典固定汇情形（链长固定）有本质区别，结构更为非平凡。转运时间和完成时链长的概率分布均呈现幂律尾部，且幂指数连续依赖于D1、D2和D3三个扩散常数。解析预测与数值模拟吻合。分数布朗运动情形下也观察到相应特征。

Conclusion: 链长波动的聚合-解聚动力学使纳米孔转运问题在定量和定性上都显著偏离固定链长情形，揭示出分裂概率和分布尾指数的连续标度行为，为理解活细胞内聚合物转运提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the translocation of a polymer chain through a nanopore where the chain length fluctuates stochastically due to the polymerization-depolymerization processes at the chain ends. We map this process to an equivalent representation where the pore performs a stochastic random-walk-like process on a line in the presence of two diffusing sinks on either side of it with diffusion constants $D_1$ and $D_3$ respectively. The translocation process terminates when the pore hits either of the two outer diffusing sinks. In the case where the pore motion itself is diffusive with diffusion constant $D_2$, we compute exactly the splitting probability that the pore hits the left (right) sink before hitting the right (left) sink. We show that the splitting probability in the presence of mobile sinks is rather nontrivial compared to the classical case of immobile sinks (the latter corresponds to the case when the chain length is fixed). Furthermore, we also compute exactly the probability distribution of the translocation time and that of the chain length at the completion time of the translocation. We show that both distributions have power law tails with exponents that depend continuously on the diffusion constants $D_1$, $D_2$ and $D_3$. We validate our analytical predictions via numerical simulations. We then present numerical results for the case when the pore performs a fractional Brownian motion with Hurst exponent $0<H<1$, while the sinks are still diffusive.

</details>


### [116] [The Memory Hidden in Response Fluctuations: Trajectory-Level Fluctuation-Response Theory and Inequalities for Non-Markovian Jump Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20328)
*Jiming Zheng,Zhiyue Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对非马尔可夫跳变过程，在观测事件记录上直接建立涨落-响应理论：每个跃迁计数满足精确的随机方程，减去历史依赖的平均倾向后余量为鞅增量；这些鞅增量构成任意可观测量随机偏差的完备正交基，其系数即事件-后果核，乘以事件强度即获得历史条件响应，导出精确的有限时间和有限频率涨落-响应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代实验常将非平衡动力学记录为离散事件序列，其速率依赖于已实现的历史。此类非马尔可夫过程因记忆效应可能破坏状态概率的闭环主方程，亟需一种能直接在观测事件记录上处理的方法，以建立通用的涨落-响应理论框架。

Method: 直接基于观测的事件记录，对每个跃迁计数建立精确随机方程。从事件中减去历史依赖的平均倾向后，剩余随机增量即为鞅增量，且不同跃迁、不同时间的鞅增量彼此正交。利用这些鞅增量构成任意可观测量（电流、占据时间、事件计数等）随机偏差的完备正交基，定义事件-后果核作为展开系数，并将该系数乘以事件强度得到历史条件响应。经过历史平均得到非负的响应-异质性间隙，并利用对数速率敏感度上界推导响应-动力学不确定性关系。

Result: 得到强度归一化响应恰好等于该事件在可观测量涨落中的展开系数，从而导出精确的有限时间和有限频率涨落-响应关系。历史平均后得到非负的响应-异质性间隙，可度量同一事件后果随历史的变化程度，并检验所提记忆坐标是否具备响应的充分性。基于对数速率敏感度上界，进一步获得受动力学活性控制的响应-动力学不确定性关系。有限历史版本可从自发轨迹中估计。

Conclusion: 该理论为非马尔可夫跳变过程提供了一套无需闭合主方程的通用涨落-响应框架，将响应刻画与涨落的鞅分解相联系，并给出可检验的间隙与不确定性界，适用于从自发轨迹估计的实际场景。

Abstract: Modern experiments often record nonequilibrium dynamics as sequences of discrete events whose rates depend on the realized past. We develop a fluctuation-response theory for such non-Markovian jump processes directly on the observed event record. Memory can destroy a closed master equation for the state probabilities. Each transition count nevertheless obeys an exact stochastic equation. After the history-dependent mean event tendency is subtracted, the remaining random increment is a martingale increment--the part of the event that cannot be predicted from the past. Martingale increments associated with different transitions and different times are orthogonal. These increments form a complete orthogonal basis for the random deviation of any observable measured from the record, such as a current, occupation time, or event count. The coefficient of a given increment is the event-consequence kernel. It measures how that event changes the predicted final observable, relative to continuing without the event, for the particular history already realized. Multiplying this kernel by the event intensity gives the history-conditioned response to perturbing the corresponding transition rate. Thus, the intensity-normalized response is exactly the expansion coefficient of that event in the observable fluctuation. This identification yields exact finite-time and finite-frequency fluctuation-response relations. Averaging over histories leaves a nonnegative response-heterogeneity gap. The gap measures how strongly the consequence of the same event varies across histories and tests whether a proposed memory coordinate is response-sufficient. Finite-history versions can be estimated from spontaneous trajectories. Bounding the logarithmic rate sensitivity further gives response-kinetic uncertainty relations controlled by dynamical activity.

</details>
